DK0208* ISSDM000060283  "Cabin Confirmation Redesign"                  *

      * ****************************************************************
      *            CABIN CONFIRMATIONS RE-DESIGN                       *
      * ****************************************************************
      * DESCRIPTION -   
      *               IN THE PROCESS OF CENTRALISING FAX/EMAIL/LETTERS  
      * FUNCTIONALITY, THIS COMMON SUB-ROUTINE IS BEING WRITTEN. ALL
      * POSSIBLE MESSAGES TO BE SENT IN NOTES SECTION OF FAX/LETTER/
      * EMAIL ARE DECLARED IN WORKING STORAGE AND AS PER ADHOC  
      * REQUIREMENTS CONTENTS FOR THE NOTES SECTION WILL BE BUILT.  
      * 
      * ANY FURTHER CHANGES IN MESSAGES ARE REQUIRED ONLY IN THIS   
      * SUB-ROUTINE AND NOT IN ALL THE FAX/EMAIL/LETTERS PROGRAMS.  
      * *************************************************************** 
      *    Areas to be opened before calling this sub-program          *
      *                                                                *
      *    CABIN-IDX           CABIN              SHIP                 *
      *    SAIL1-IDX           SAIL1              PRICING              *
      *    CITY-NAME           CITY-SAL-IDX       CITY-SAIL            *
      *    AGENCY-ASSOC        AGENT-IDX          AGENT                *
      *    TOUR-PACKAGE        PAX-PKG            PAXXFER              *
214567*    CONFIRM             GRP1-BOOK                               *
      ******************************************************************
      * 
      * INPUTS REQUIRED FOR THIS SUB-ROUTINE -  
      *          1. BOOKING NUMBER  
      *          2. GROUP BOOKING NUMBER (OPTIONAL) 
      *          2. VIEW TYPE (C - GUEST; A - AGENT AND B - BOTH)   
      *          3. CONFIRMATION NUMBER 
      * 
      * OUTPUT OF THE SUB-ROUTINE - 
      *                             CONTENTS OF NOTE SECTION TO BE SENT 
      * THRU FAX OR EMAIL OR LETTER/MAIL.   
      * 
      * CALLING PROGRAMS AS OF NOW ARE -
      *          1. CCR73CLC (FOR EMAILS)   
      *          2. IRR70FAX (FOR FAX)  
      *          3. IRR20RCS (FOR LETTERS)  
      * 
      ****************************************************************  
      * 
      * INPUT     DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS  
      *           RSP73-EMAIL-CONTENT-INPUT 
      * 
      * OUTPUT    RSP73-EMAIL-CONTENT-OUTPUT
      * 
      * CALLING SEQUENCE -  
      * 
      *       CALL 'RSO73GCF' USING DPP01-COMMON-DATA-FIELDS
      *                             DPP01-COMMON-INFO   
      *                             RSP73-EMAIL-CONTENT-INPUT   
      *                             RSP73-EMAIL-CONTENT-OUTPUT  
      * 
      * Return Status Values:                                        *  
      *                00       OK                                   ** 
      *                01       Informational                        ** 
      *                02       RPC Error                            ** 
      *                03       Fatal or Severe Error                ** 
      ******************************************************************

       ENVIRONMENT DIVISION.
       CONFIGURATION SECTION.   
       SOURCE-COMPUTER.           UNISYS.   
       OBJECT-COMPUTER. UNISYS-2200 MEMORY 3 MODULES.   
       SPECIAL-NAMES.   
           PRINTER IS PRINTER   
           CONSOLE IS CONSOLE.  

       DATA DIVISION.   

       SUBSCHEMA SECTION.   
       INVOKE SUBSCHEMA PST100UCOBSU

C90569 3500-PROCESS-CFR-RECORDS.
C90569     MOVE 0      TO WS-CFR-IND.

C90569     MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SUB.

C90569     MOVE 'N' TO WS-CFR-BK-IND.   
C90569******************************************************************
C90569*    Fetch confirmation record. If no find get out of this process
C90569*    There is no associated confirmation record with this booking.
C90569*    If a record exists then process for history and modify it.   
C90569*    If error other than find or no find then email error and get 
C90569*    of this process. 
C90569******************************************************************
C90569     MOVE RSP73-I-INDV-BOOK-NUM TO CFR-BOOK-NUM.  
C90569     MOVE 2 TO FETCH-WITH-LOCK.   
C90569     FETCH CONFIRMATION-REC RECORD
C90569           ON ERROR GO TO 3500-ERROR-CHECK.   
C90569     MOVE 0 TO FETCH-WITH-LOCK.   
C90569     GO TO 3500-PROCESS-CFR1. 

C90569 3500-ERROR-CHECK.
C90569     MOVE 0 TO FETCH-WITH-LOCK.   
C90569     IF ERROR-NUM = '0006' OR '0007' OR '0013'
C90569*    no confirmation records for this booking 
C90569        GO TO 3500-EXIT   
C90569     ELSE 
C90569*       this is a problem 
C90569        PERFORM 3515-CFR-ERROR THRU 3515-EXIT 
C90569        GO TO 3500-EXIT   
C90569     END-IF.  

C90569******************************************************************
C90569*     done with retrieving confirmation record
C90569*     MODIFY THE CFR-PROCESS-IND. 
C90569*     determine if confirm records are found  
C90569******************************************************************

C90569 3500-PROCESS-CFR1.   
C90569*    If record has process indicator already set then get out 
C90569     IF CFR-PROCESS-IND = 1   
C90569        GO TO 3500-EXIT.  

C90569     MOVE CFR-ACTIVITY-CODE TO WS-CFR-ACTIVITY-CODE.  
C90569*    Set the process ind in the confirmation record   
C90569     PERFORM 3501-MODIFY-IND THRU 3501-EXIT.  
C90569*    Check for dups and if activity is same and process is set
C90569*    then delete that dup and set inicator to modify second   
C90569*    indicator in the original record fetched.
C90569     PERFORM 3505-FETCH-DUP-CFR THRU 3505-EXIT.   

C90569     IF WS-CFR-CNT > 0
C90569*       confirmation records found
C90569*       see if new booking or paymnent activity code found
C90569*       if no code found then leave process   
C90569*       if found determine what history comment to use
C90569        IF WS-CFR-PAY-IND = 'N' AND WS-CFR-BK-IND = 'N'   
C90569*          no activities found that match our criteria
C90569           GO TO 3500-EXIT
C90569        END-IF
C90569*       figure out what notes to use for VB   
C90569        PERFORM 3502-DETERMINE-COMMENT THRU 3502-EXIT 
C90569     END-IF.  

C90569 3500-EXIT.   
C90569     EXIT.

C90569 3501-MODIFY-IND. 
C90569     MOVE 1 TO CFR-PROCESS-IND.   
C90569     MODIFY CONFIRMATION-REC RECORD   
C90569         ON ERROR GO TO 3501-CHECK-ERROR. 
c90569     FREE.
C90569     GO TO 3501-EXIT. 
C90569 3501-CHECK-ERROR.
C90569     DISPLAY 'MODIFY PROCESS-IND ERROR ' UPON PRINTER.
C90569     PERFORM 3515-CFR-ERROR THRU 3515-EXIT.   

C90569 3501-EXIT.   
C90569     EXIT.

C90569 3502-DETERMINE-COMMENT.  
C90569     MOVE SPACES TO WS-CFR-COND.  
C90569     IF WS-CFR-PAY-IND = 'Y' AND WS-CFR-BK-IND = 'Y'  
C90569*       both types of activities found
C90569        MOVE 'B' TO WS-CFR-COND   
C90569     ELSE 
C90569        IF WS-CFR-PAY-IND = 'Y' AND WS-CFR-BK-IND = 'N'   
C90569*       only payment activity found   
C90569           MOVE 'P' TO WS-CFR-COND
C90569        ELSE  
C90569           IF WS-CFR-PAY-IND = 'N' AND WS-CFR-BK-IND = 'Y'
C90569*          only new booking activity found
C90569              MOVE 'N' TO WS-CFR-COND 
C90569           END-IF 
C90569        END-IF
C90569     END-IF.  
C90569  
C90569 3502-EXIT.   
C90569     EXIT.

C90569******************************************************************
C90569*    See what activity code and set up for proper comments in 
C90569*     history rec. If it is not the correct activity code keep
C90569*     fetching next dup. Only activity codes of 'CRC' or 'ACB' OR 
C90569*     'NBK' OR 'PNI' OR 'PNA' get processed.  
C90569*     There can be more than one Confirmation record  
C90569*     for this booking (payment received or new booking   
C90569*     confirmation) so look for duplicates.  We continue until no 
C90569*     more duplicates are found. Based on what is found we set up 
C90569*     what comments are to be written to history. 
C90569******************************************************************
C90569 3505-FETCH-DUP-CFR.  
C90569     IF CFR-ACTIVITY-CODE = 'CRC' 
C90569        MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CFR-PAY-IND
C90569        ADD 1 TO WS-CFR-CNT   
C90569     ELSE 
C90569        IF CFR-ACTIVITY-CODE = 'ACB' OR 'NBK' OR 'PNI' OR 'PNA'   
C90569           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-CFR-BK-IND  
C90569           ADD 1 TO WS-CFR-CNT
C90569        ELSE  
C90569*          confirmation record does not meet activity criteria
C90569           CONTINUE   
C90569        END-IF
C90569     END-IF.  

C90569     FETCH NEXT DUPLICATE CONFIRMATION-REC RECORD 
C90569               ON ERROR GO TO 3505-ERROR-CHECK.   
C90569     IF CFR-ACTIVITY-CODE = 'ACB' OR 'NBK' OR 'PNI' OR 'PNA'  
C90569                               OR 'CRC'   
C90569        PERFORM 3510-MOD-CFR-REC THRU 3510-EXIT   
C90569     END-IF.  
C90569     GO TO 3505-FETCH-DUP-CFR.

C90569 3505-ERROR-CHECK.
C90569     IF ERROR-NUM = '0006' OR '0007' OR '0013'
C90569*       no more confirmation records  
C90569        GO TO 3505-EXIT   
C90569     ELSE 
C90569*       problem with confirmation record  
C90569        PERFORM 3515-CFR-ERROR THRU 3515-EXIT 
C90569     END-IF.  
C90569  
C90569 3505-EXIT.   
C90569     EXIT.
C90569  

C90569 3507-EXIT.   
C90569     EXIT.
C90569  
C90569 3510-MOD-CFR-REC.
C90569     MOVE 1 TO CFR-PROCESS-IND.   
C90569     MODIFY CONFIRMATION-REC RECORD   
C90569         ON ERROR GO TO 3510-CHECK-ERROR. 
C90569     FREE.
C90569     GO TO 3510-EXIT. 
C90569 3510-CHECK-ERROR.
C90569     DISPLAY 'Modify CFR-PROCESS-IND' UPON PRINTER.   
C90569     PERFORM 3515-CFR-ERROR THRU 3515-EXIT.   

C90569 3510-EXIT.   
C90569     EXIT.

C90569******************************************************************
C90569*    Display any errors relating to processing confirmation reocrds   
C90569******************************************************************
C90569 3515-CFR-ERROR.  
C90569*    MOVE 0 TO MCFLAG.

C90569     DISPLAY 'RSP73-BOOK-NUM     : ' RSP73-I-INDV-BOOK-NUM
C90569                                                     UPON PRINTER.

PPC470*6479-FIND-ODD-CHARACTER. 
PPC470*    SUBTRACT 1  FROM WS-POS. 
PPC470*    COMPUTE WS-MSG-LENGTH = WS-POS * 80  
PPC470*    MOVE 1 TO WS-POS.
PPC470*    ADD 1                  TO WS-MSGCNT. 
PPC470*    MOVE 'GN'              TO RSP73-O-NOTE-TYPE (WS-MSGCNT).

PPC470*       BLANK-TEXT-TYPE

PPC470*    ELSE 
PPC470*        MOVE 'N'           TO RSP73-O-EMAIL-DISP-IND (WS-MSGCNT) 
PPC470*    END-IF.  
PPC470*    MOVE SPACES           TO RSP73-O-NOTE-DESCRIPTION(WS-MSGCNT).
PPC470* 
PPC470*    INSPECT WS-PAITR-TEXT REPLACING ALL ' ' BY '\'.  
PPC470*    PERFORM VARYING WS-TSUB FROM 1 BY 1  
PPC470*                       UNTIL WS-TSUB > WS-MSG-LENGTH 
PPC470*       IF WS-PAITR-TEXT (WS-TSUB:1) < SPACE  
PPC470*          MOVE SPACES TO WS-PAITR-NOTE   
PPC470*          COMPUTE WS-NOTE-LENGTH = (WS-TSUB - WS-POS)
PPC470*          MOVE WS-PAITR-TEXT (WS-POS:WS-NOTE-LENGTH) 
PPC470*                                    TO WS-PAITR-NOTE 
PPC470*          IF WS-PAITR-NOTE > SPACES  
PPC470*             PERFORM 6458-SEND-GUEST-NOTE
PPC470*          END-IF 
PPC470*          COMPUTE WS-POS = WS-POS + WS-NOTE-LENGTH + 1   
PPC470*       END-IF
PPC470*    END-PERFORM. 
PPC470*    MOVE WS-PAITR-TEXT (WS-POS: ) TO WS-PAITR-NOTE.  
PPC470*    PERFORM 6458-SEND-GUEST-NOTE.
PPC470*    INSPECT RSP73-O-NOTE-DESCRIPTION(WS-MSGCNT)  
PPC470*                                  REPLACING ALL '\' BY ' '.  
PPC470*    MOVE WS-PMR1-DBKEY TO GENERIC-DBK
PPC470*    FETCH PRICING-MASTER-REC RECORD GENERIC-DBK. 
PPC470*    GO TO 6470-PAX-PRICE-LOOP.

PPC470*                   MOVE WS-BV-UNIT-MESG-1
PPC470*                                TO  WS-MESG-TEMP 
PPC470*                   IF (WS-INFANT-IND = 'N')               AND
PPC470*                      (RSP06-PAX-AGE (WS-SUB-Y)  NOT <   
PPC470*                       PMR1-BV-ITEM-MIN-AGE) 
PPC470*                       ADD 1    TO WS-SUB-Z  
PPC470* IN CASE OF PER GUEST, WS-SUB-Z IS COUNT OF VALID GUESTS 
PPC470*                   ELSE  
PPC470*                       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-BV-DISCL-FLAG  
PPC470*                   END-IF
PPC470*                       ADD 1    TO WS-PAX-SUB
PPC470*                       MOVE RSP06-PAX-SEQ-NUM (WS-SUB-Y) 
PPC470*                                TO WS-BV-PAX-SEQ-NUM (WS-PAX-SUB)
PPC470*               END-IF
PPC470*            END-IF   
PPC470*        END-IF   
PPC470*    END-PERFORM. 
PPC470* 
PPC470*    IF ((PMR1-BV-ITEM-UNIT = 'P') AND (WS-SUB-Z > ZEROES)) OR
PPC470*       ((PMR1-BV-ITEM-UNIT = 'C') AND
PPC470*        (WS-SUB-Z < WS-TOTAL-BV-PAX-CNT))
PPC470*         MOVE 1                 TO BVP05-ITEM-CNT
PPC470*         MOVE PMR1-BV-ITEM-CODE TO   
PPC470*              BVP05-ITEM-NUM (BVP05-ITEM-CNT)

PPC470*                               DPP01-COMMON-INFO 
PPC470*                               BVP05-INPUT-DATA  
PPC470*                               BVP05-OUTPUT-DATA 
PPC470* 
PPC470*         MOVE BVP05-ITEM-DESC (1)
PPC470*                                TO WS-BV-ITEM-DESC   
PPC470*    ELSE 
PPC470*        MOVE 'N'                TO WS-BV-MESG-IND
PPC470*    END-IF.  
PPC470* 
PPC470*6200-BV-ITEM-CHECKS-EXIT.
PPC470*    EXIT.

159470 6500-GET-TWR-REC.
159470     MOVE RSP06-IBR-TRVL-WITH-NUM TO TWR-TRVL-WITH-NUM.   
159470     MOVE RSP06-IBR-SHIP-CODE TO TWR-SHIP-CODE.   
159470     MOVE RSP06-IBR-SAIL-DATE TO TWR-SAIL-DATE.   
159470     MOVE RSP06-IBR-DURATION-DAYS TO TWR-DURATION-DAYS.   
159470     MOVE SPACES TO WS-DINING-BKG-NUMBERS.
159470     MOVE 1 TO WS-DIN-SUB.
159470     FETCH TRAVEL-WITH-REC RECORD.
159470     PERFORM VARYING WS-TWR-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
159470                     UNTIL WS-TWR-SUB > TWR-ENTRY-CTR 
159470         IF TWR-BOOK-STATUS (WS-TWR-SUB) NOT = SPACE  
159470            CONTINUE  
159470         ELSE 
159470            IF TWR-BOOK-NUM (WS-TWR-SUB) = RSP06-IBR-BOOK-NUM 
159470               CONTINUE   
159470            ELSE  
159470               MOVE TWR-BOOK-NUM (WS-TWR-SUB) TO  
159470                         WS-DINING-BKG-NUM (WS-DIN-SUB)   
159470               MOVE ',' TO WS-DINING-BKG-COMMA (WS-DIN-SUB)   
159470            ADD 1 TO WS-DIN-SUB   
159470            END-IF
159470         END-IF   
159470     END-PERFORM. 
161405**Ensure WS-DIN-SUB large enough not to fail run-check.   
161405     IF WS-DIN-SUB > 1
159470     MOVE '.' TO WS-DINING-BKG-COMMA (WS-DIN-SUB - 1).
159470 6500-EXIT.   
159470     EXIT.
